<a id="category-1"></a>
## Category 1 — Bullets

ATT category 1 displays **Bullets**. The English source comment records the singular ItemSubClass name “Bullet” and an ATT pluralization. The Italian row has a specific Retail fallback because the other queried flavor rows returned English text.

### Locale / name / ATT condition

| Locale | Name / value in ATT | ATT condition |
| --- | --- | --- |
| en | `"Bullets"` | Unconditional |
| de | `"Kugel"` | Unconditional |
| es | `"Bala"` | Unconditional |
| mx | `"Bala"` | Unconditional |
| fr | `"Balle"` | Unconditional |
| it | `"Pallottola"` | Unconditional |
| ko | `"탄환"` | Unconditional |
| pt | `"Projétil"` | Unconditional |
| ru | `"Пули"` | Unconditional |
| cn | `"子弹"` | Unconditional |
| tw | `"子彈"` | Unconditional |

### Native-name source

All locale comments reference `ItemSubClass[29].DisplayName_lang`. The directly found scopes below are recorded separately for each locale; grouping reflects identical evidence, not merely identical displayed names.

| Locales | Found source flavors | Reading note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| en | RETAIL, RETAIL PTR, CLASSIC ERA, TBC PTR, TBC ANNIVERSARY, MOP CLASSIC, FOREVER BETA, TITAN BETA (source-only), SEASON OF DISCOVERY | The raw source is “Bullet”; ATT pluralizes it as “Bullets.” |
| de, es, mx, fr, ko, pt, ru, cn, tw | RETAIL, RETAIL PTR, CLASSIC ERA, TBC PTR, TBC ANNIVERSARY, MOP CLASSIC, FOREVER BETA, TITAN BETA (source-only), SEASON OF DISCOVERY | Each comment records the same native-name source and scope. |
| it | RETAIL, RETAIL PTR | The other targets use the fallback described below. |

### Fallback and query results

For Italian, ItemSubClass[29].DisplayName_lang was directly found in RETAIL and RETAIL PTR. The other recorded targets are CLASSIC ERA, TBC PTR, TBC ANNIVERSARY, MOP CLASSIC, FOREVER BETA, TITAN BETA (source-only), and SEASON OF DISCOVERY. Those requested itIT rows returned the English fallback “Bullet”; “Pallottola” is borrowed from Retail itIT. The comment records fallback and unsuccessful localized-name lookup separately.

### Semantic relations
